- **o2-coder**: The coder in the o2-loop review loop. Implements a confirmed spec in the current checkout, runs the repo's gates, and answers reviewer findings round by round. Spawned by the orchestrating session with a ledger path; continued across rounds with SendMessage so it keeps its context. Never commits. ([openobserve/openobserve](https://github.com/openobserve/openobserve/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/o2-coder.md) · ★23k · 2 copies)
- **o3-eval-runner**: Runs one eval case from `tools/authoring-skill/evals/` — the prompt as written, the scripted persona standing in for the human — captures the transcript and the artifacts, applies the case's graders, and emits judged JSON. Use it to check what a skill in the o3sanity plugin actually makes an agent do, before and after a change. ([o3world/o3-sanity](https://github.com/o3world/o3-sanity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/o3-eval-runner.md))
- **o55-builder-precise**: Opus 5.5 builder on extra high, for precision engineering. Use when a sub-task connects several parts, makes design decisions inside the plan, touches shared state, concurrency, a wire format, money, auth, permissions or a data migration, or when an earlier builder already failed on it. The router confirms or changes the pick per sub-task. Edits files and runs tests by itself. ([stas4000/claude-subagent-router](https://github.com/stas4000/claude-subagent-router/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/o55-builder-precise.md) · ★10)
- **o55-reviewer**: Opus 5.5 technical reviewer on high, the final gate of every real build. Use after the verifier: judges the diff for correctness, regressions, scope and maintainability, reruns the checks and returns PASS or FAIL with exact fixes. Read-only, never edits files. ([stas4000/claude-subagent-router](https://github.com/stas4000/claude-subagent-router/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/o55-reviewer.md) · ★10)

- **oag-capture**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for measurement lanes on a running original - driving PPSSPP, PCSX2 or RPCS3 to capture frames, traces, memory watchpoints or menu walks, and turning a question into a measured number or a reference image. Use when a lane's answer has to come from the original running, not from static reading. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-capture.md) · ★36)
- **oag-compact**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for the comment compact lane - one crate per member, rewriting in-code comments only (`//`, `///`, `//!`) to cut what restates the code or narrates history, keep the why, and leave every line of code byte-identical. Use for the standing sequential compact lane. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-compact.md) · ★36)
- **oag-docs**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for documentation and handover lanes - auditing status.md, handover threads and docs pages against the code and the evidence, fixing stale claims, closing landed threads, and consolidating findings. Use when a lane changes no code logic. The lead may spawn it with model haiku for purely mechanical sweeps. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-docs.md) · ★36)
- **oag-format**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for asset and container format lanes - decoding a binary file family (models, textures, collision, sound banks, archives, tables) from the discs, proving the reader against every file that ships, and documenting the layout. Use when a lane's main output is a parser. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-format.md) · ★36)
- **oag-perf**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for performance and profiling lanes - measuring where time, memory or bytes go (frame time, load time, allocations, type and snapshot sizes), finding the cause with a profiler, making the fix, and proving the number moved with a before and after. Use when a lane's goal is a measured speed, memory or size win, not a feature. Unlike oag-scout it changes code. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-perf.md) · ★36)
- **oag-re**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for reverse-engineering lanes - decompiling functions in Ghidra, naming them with confidence scores, tracing a behaviour to its instruction-level law, and writing the evidence pages. Use when a lane's main output is recovered behaviour from an original executable. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-re.md) · ★36)
- **oag-scout**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for read-only opportunity scouting - sweeping a subsystem for one class of improvement (render performance, memory allocations, and the like), ranking what it finds with evidence and a way to measure each, and writing it into a handover thread. Changes no code. Cheap by design: spawned on haiku, 0 slots; its findings are checked by its advisor before it reports. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-scout.md) · ★36)
- **oag-wire**: OpenAntiGrav drive member for implementation lanes whose reverse-engineering is already done - wiring a recovered law, an already-decoded asset or an existing mechanism into the simulation, renderer, front end or audio so a player sees or hears it. Use for ready-to-wire handover threads. ([topaxi/OpenAntiGrav](https://github.com/topaxi/OpenAntiGrav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oag-wire.md) · ★36)
- **oakmonte-researcher**: Web and market researcher for Oakmonte — SEO keyword and search-intent research for Nigerian and African fashion, cosmetics and arts sellers, competitor and platform comparisons (Shopify, Bumpa, Paystack, Shipbubble, Instagram commerce), ad-platform and policy lookups, and fact-checking marketing claims against what the code actually does. Read-only; reports findings with sources, does not edit files. Use before writing a /learn guide, an off-site article, or ad copy, and whenever a number or claim about a third party needs checking. Use for review of api.* routes, anything touching store_credentials or store scoping, schema writes, importer logic, or before merging changes to auth, data access, or export. - **oauth-discovery**: The number-one reason a remote MCP server "won't connect" is not that it's down — it's that a client hitting it unauthenticated gets a dead-end response instead of a machine-followable pointer to where auth starts. The fix is a three-hop discovery chain: a `401` carrying `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer resource_metadata=...`, a protected-resource metadata document (RFC 9728), and an authorization-server ([ever-just/discoverability](https://github.com/ever-just/discoverability/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/oauth-discovery.md))

- **obrigacoes-calendario**: Monta a agenda de obrigações e vencimentos por cliente, regime e mês (DAS, DARF, FGTS Digital, eSocial, EFD-Reinf, DCTFWeb, SPED e anuais), com a regra de antecipar ou prorrogar de cada obrigação quando o prazo cai em fim de semana ou feriado, e gera o calendário do mês em CSV. Use ao planejar o mês da carteira ou conferir um prazo. Não use para apurar tributos nem transmitir declarações (obrigacoes-dctfweb-reinf). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/obrigacoes-calendario.md) · ★1)
- **obrigacoes-dctfweb-reinf**: Prepara e confere a EFD-Reinf (R-1000, R-1070, R-2010, R-2020, R-2055, R-4010, R-4020, R-2099 e R-4099) e a DCTFWeb com MIT, conciliando retido nas notas, declarado e DARF numerado pago, com script entre planilhas. Use ao receber notas com retenção, relatórios da Reinf ou do eSocial, recibo da DCTFWeb ou DARF com divergência. Não use para calcular retenções (fiscal-retencoes-federais) nem folha (dp-folha-mensal). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/obrigacoes-dctfweb-reinf.md) · ★1)
- **obrigacoes-declaracoes-anuais**: Define as declarações anuais de cada cliente pelo regime e pela atividade: DEFIS, DASN-SIMEI, DIMOB, DMED, informes de rendimentos, situação da extinta DIRF, RAIS × eSocial e declarações estaduais e municipais, com checklist e prazos. Use ao planejar o fechamento do ano, receber pedido de DEFIS, DMED ou DIMOB ou revisar a carteira. Não use para ECD e ECF (obrigacoes-ecd-ecf) nem agenda mensal (obrigacoes-calendario). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/obrigacoes-declaracoes-anuais.md) · ★1)
- **obrigacoes-ecd-ecf**: Confere a ECD (diário, saldos, BP, DRE, assinaturas e substituição) e a ECF (blocos 0, C, E, J, K, L, M, N, P, X e Y), com cruzamentos e script que confere o I155. Use ao receber TXT da ECD ou da ECF, balancete anual, e-Lalur, recibo ou erro do PVA, ou dúvida sobre obrigatoriedade e prazos. Não use para calcular IRPJ/CSLL (fiscal-lucro-real, fiscal-lucro-presumido) nem montar demonstrações (contabil-demonstracoes). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/obrigacoes-ecd-ecf.md) · ★1)
- **obrigacoes-efd-contribuicoes**: Revisa e valida a EFD-Contribuições: blocos 0, A, C, D, F, M, P e 1, apuração no M200/M600, controle de créditos (1100/1500) e regime de caixa do Presumido (F500/F550), com script que confere o bloco M contra os documentos. Use ao receber o TXT da EFD-Contribuições, log do PVA ou dúvida sobre prazo e dispensa. Não use para calcular créditos de insumos (fiscal-pis-cofins) nem SPED Fiscal (obrigacoes-efd-icms-ipi). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/obrigacoes-efd-contribuicoes.md) · ★1)
- **obrigacoes-efd-icms-ipi**: Monta, revisa e valida a EFD ICMS/IPI (SPED Fiscal): 0000, 0150, 0200, C100/C170/C190, D100, E110, E200, E520, G110, H010, K200/K230 e 1010, com C190 × E110 em Python, inventário, bloco K e erros do PVA. Use ao receber o TXT do SPED Fiscal, log do PVA, inventário de fim de ano ou pedido de retificação. Não use para calcular ICMS ou ST (fiscal-icms) nem para EFD-Contribuições (obrigacoes-efd-contribuicoes). Pick when a PR / design adds layers, interfaces, factories, base classes, or new vocabulary that the requirement did not ask for.
